Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any elliptic module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 213. The projective resolution has length at most 700 with Betti numbers bounded by 5.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 213 + O(1/n^700). Summing over all modes and applying the elliptic convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
